6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

       d. Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml

    type : BookItemType
}

    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem

    status : LibraryItemStatus
}

    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void

}

    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}

    DISC
}

    VIDEO

    COMPLETED
```

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary:
  Counts the total number of print-format book items contained in this
  order, but only when the order's status is COMPLETED. Returns 0 when
  the order is not completed or when no print-format book items exist.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
       a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
       b. If item is a BookItem AND item.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
             - count += itemLine.quantity
  4. Return count.
Related Features:
  - Order.status (OrderStatus)
  - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
  - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
  - ItemLine.quantity (int)
Related Classes:
  Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, OrderStatus, BookItemType
Input:
  (none) - operates on the receiver order's current state.
Output:
  int
    - The summed quantity of PRINT_FORMAT book items in this order when
      the order is COMPLETED. Returns 0 when the order is not COMPLETED
      or when there are no print-format book items.
Pre-condition:
  - The receiver Order object must be initialized with a non-null
    itemLines list (possibly empty) and a valid status.
Post-condition:
  - No state is modified. Returns a non-negative integer.

Order::addItemLine
Summary:
  Adds a new item line to this order. Validates that the order is in
  PENDING status, that the referenced library item is AVAILABLE, and that
  no duplicate item line (same library item) already exists in the order.
  For PRINT_FORMAT book items or DISC digital items, the library item's
  status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD; otherwise the item's status
  remains AVAILABLE. Returns true on success, false on any validation
  failure.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING, return false.
  2. If itemLine is null, return false.
  3. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
  4. If item is null or item.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
  5. For each existing line in this.itemLines:
       - If line.libraryItem == item, return false (duplicate).
  6. Append itemLine to this.itemLines.
  7. Determine if the item requires a hold:
       - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
       - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
       - requiresHold = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
  8. If requiresHold, set item.status = HOLD.
     Otherwise, leave item.status as AVAILABLE.
  9. Return true.
Related Features:
  - Order.status (OrderStatus)
  - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
  - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
  - LibraryItem.status (LibraryItemStatus)
Related Classes:
  Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus,
  LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input:
  itemLine : ItemLine
    - The item line to be added. Must be non-null and contain a non-null
      libraryItem whose status is AVAILABLE. The quantity is assumed to be
      a positive integer (>= 1).
Output:
  boolean
    - true  : item line was successfully added (and library item may have
              been moved to HOLD).
    - false : validation failed; no state was modified.
Pre-condition:
  - this Order must be in PENDING status.
  - itemLine must be non-null and its libraryItem must be AVAILABLE.
  - No existing item line in this order may reference the same libraryItem.
Post-condition:
  - On success: itemLine is appended to this.itemLines; if the library item
    is a PRINT_FORMAT book or DISC digital, its status becomes HOLD;
    otherwise it remains AVAILABLE.
  - On failure: no state changes occur.

Order::removeItemLine
Summary:
  Removes an item line from this order when the order is in PENDING
  status. If the removed library item was on HOLD (and is a PRINT_FORMAT
  book item or DISC digital item), its status is reset to AVAILABLE;
  otherwise the item's status is left unchanged. Returns the updated
  count of item lines in the order on success, or -1 if the order is
  not in PENDING status.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING, return -1.
  2. If itemLine is null or not found in this.itemLines, return the
     current size of this.itemLines (no change).
  3. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
  4. Remove itemLine from this.itemLines.
  5. Determine if the item requires status reset:
       - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
       - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
       - isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
  6. If isPrintOrDisc AND item.status == HOLD:
       - Set item.status = AVAILABLE.
     Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
  7. Return this.itemLines.size().
Related Features:
  - Order.status (OrderStatus)
  - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
  - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
  - LibraryItem.status (LibraryItemStatus)
Related Classes:
  Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus,
  LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input:
  itemLine : ItemLine
    - The item line to be removed. Must be non-null and currently present
      in this.itemLines.
Output:
  int
    - On success: the updated count of item lines in this order (>= 0).
    - On failure (order not PENDING): -1.
Pre-condition:
  - this Order must be in PENDING status.
  - itemLine must be non-null and exist within this.itemLines.
Post-condition:
  - On success: itemLine is removed from this.itemLines; if the removed
    library item was a PRINT_FORMAT book or DISC digital in HOLD status,
    its status is reset to AVAILABLE.
  - On failure (order not PENDING): no state changes occur and -1 is
    returned.

Order::handleOrder
Summary:
  Finalizes an existing order by transitioning its status to COMPLETED
  and then updating the status of each library item that was previously
  placed on HOLD (only for PRINT_FORMAT book items and DISC digital
  items) to LOAN. Other item types retain their current status.
Algorithm:
  1. Set this.status = COMPLETED.
  2. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
       a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
       b. Determine if the item is a print/disc item:
            - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
            - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
            - isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
       c. If isPrintOrDisc AND item.status == HOLD:
             - Set item.status = LOAN.
       d. Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
  3. End.
Related Features:
  - Order.status (OrderStatus)
  - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
  - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
  - LibraryItem.status (LibraryItemStatus)
Related Classes:
  Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus,
  LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input:
  (none) - operates on the receiver order's current state.
Output:
  void
    - No return value.
Pre-condition:
  - The receiver Order must be initialized with a non-null itemLines list
    and a valid status (typically PENDING, though the operation will set
    COMPLETED regardless of current status to satisfy the requirement).
Post-condition:
  - this.status == COMPLETED.
  - For every PRINT_FORMAT book or DISC digital item previously in HOLD
    status, its status is now LOAN. All other items retain their previous
    status.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary:
  Calculates the total number of downloads for this specific Audio digital
  item across all Completed orders. Only orders whose status is COMPLETED
  are considered, and only when this digital item's option is DOWNLOADABLE
  (not DISC). Returns 0 when there are no qualifying downloads.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.type != AUDIO, return 0 (only audio items are downloadable).
  2. If this.option != DOWNLOADABLE, return 0 (disc items do not count).
  3. Initialize total = 0.
  4. For each order in orders:
       a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip.
       b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
             i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this:
                   - total += itemLine.quantity
  5. Return total.
Related Features:
  - DigitalItem.option (DigitalItemOption)
  - DigitalItem.type (DigitalItemType)
Related Classes:
  DigitalItem, Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
Input:
  orders : List<Order>
    - A non-null list of Order objects to be inspected. May be empty.
Output:
  int
    - The total quantity of this digital item appearing in item lines of
      Completed orders, provided this item is an AUDIO item with option
      DOWNLOADABLE. Returns 0 if no qualifying downloads exist or if this
      item is not eligible (non-audio or disc option).
Pre-condition:
  - orders must not be null (may be empty).
  - this DigitalItem must be a valid object.
  - this DigitalItem's type should be AUDIO and option should be DOWNLOADABLE
    for a non-zero result.
Post-condition:
  - No state is modified. The method returns a non-negative integer count.

```java
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Adds a new item line to this order. Validates that the order is in
   *   PENDING status, that the referenced library item is AVAILABLE, and that
   *   no duplicate item line (same library item) already exists in the order.
   *   For PRINT_FORMAT book items or DISC digital items, the library item's
   *   status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD; otherwise the item's status
   *   remains AVAILABLE. Returns true on success, false on any validation
   *   failure.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.status != PENDING, return false.
   *   2. If itemLine is null, return false.
   *   3. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   *   4. If item is null or item.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
   *   5. For each existing line in this.itemLines:
   *        - If line.libraryItem == item, return false (duplicate).
   *   6. Append itemLine to this.itemLines.
   *   7. Determine if the item requires a hold:
   *        - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
   *        - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
   *        - requiresHold = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
   *   8. If requiresHold, set item.status = HOLD.
   *      Otherwise, leave item.status as AVAILABLE.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features:
   *   - Order.status (OrderStatus)
   *   - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
   *   - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
   *   - LibraryItem.status (LibraryItemStatus)
   * Related Classes:
   *   Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus,
   *   LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
   * Input:
   *   itemLine : ItemLine
   *     - The item line to be added. Must be non-null and contain a non-null
   *       libraryItem whose status is AVAILABLE. The quantity is assumed to be
   *       a positive integer (>= 1).
   * Output:
   *   boolean
   *     - true  : item line was successfully added (and library item may have
   *               been moved to HOLD).
   *     - false : validation failed; no state was modified.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - this Order must be in PENDING status.
   *   - itemLine must be non-null and its libraryItem must be AVAILABLE.
   *   - No existing item line in this order may reference the same libraryItem.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - On success: itemLine is appended to this.itemLines; if the library item
   *     is a PRINT_FORMAT book or DISC digital, its status becomes HOLD;
   *     otherwise it remains AVAILABLE.
   *   - On failure: no state changes occur.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->

   *   otherwise the item's status is left unchanged. Returns the updated

   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.status != PENDING, return -1.

   *   4. Remove itemLine from this.itemLines.
   *   5. Determine if the item requires status reset:
   *        - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)

   *        - isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
   *   6. If isPrintOrDisc AND item.status == HOLD:
   *        - Set item.status = AVAILABLE.
   *      Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
   *   7. Return this.itemLines.size().
   * Related Features:
```

   * Summary:
   *   Adds a new item line to this order. Validates that the order is in
   *   PENDING status, that the referenced library item is AVAILABLE, and that
   *   no duplicate item line (same library item) already exists in the order.
   *   For PRINT_FORMAT book items or DISC digital items, the library item's
   *   status is changed from AVAILABLE to HOLD; otherwise the item's status
   *   remains AVAILABLE. Returns true on success, false on any validation
   *   failure.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.status != PENDING, return false.
   *   2. If itemLine is null, return false.
   *   3. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   *   4. If item is null or item.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
   *   5. For each existing line in this.itemLines:
   *        - If line.libraryItem == item, return false (duplicate).
   *   6. Append itemLine to this.itemLines.
   *   7. Determine if the item requires a hold:
   *        - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
   *        - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
   *        - requiresHold = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
   *   8. If requiresHold, set item.status = HOLD.
   *      Otherwise, leave item.status as AVAILABLE.
   *   9. Return true.
   * Related Features:

  public boolean addItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (itemLine == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    final LibraryItem item = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    if (item == null || item.getStatus() != LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE)
    {
      return false;
    }
    for (final ItemLine line : getItemLines())
    {
      if (line.getLibraryItem() == item)
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    getItemLines().add(itemLine);
    final boolean isPrintBook = (item instanceof BookItem) && (((BookItem) item).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT);
    final boolean isDiscDigital = (item instanceof DigitalItem) && (((DigitalItem) item).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC);
    final boolean requiresHold = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital;
    if (requiresHold)
    {
      item.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.HOLD);
    }
    return true;
  }

   * Summary:
   *   Removes an item line from this order when the order is in PENDING
   *   status. If the removed library item was on HOLD (and is a PRINT_FORMAT
   *   book item or DISC digital item), its status is reset to AVAILABLE;
   *   otherwise the item's status is left unchanged. Returns the updated

   * Algorithm:

   *   6. If isPrintOrDisc AND item.status == HOLD:

   *     - On failure (order not PENDING or itemLine not found): -1.

   *     occur and -1 is returned.

  public int removeItemLine(final ItemLine itemLine)
  {
    if (getStatus() != OrderStatus.PENDING)
    {
      return -1;
    }
    if (itemLine == null || !getItemLines().contains(itemLine))
    {
      return -1;
    }
    final LibraryItem item = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
    getItemLines().remove(itemLine);
    final boolean isPrintBook = (item instanceof BookItem) && (((BookItem) item).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT);
    final boolean isDiscDigital = (item instanceof DigitalItem) && (((DigitalItem) item).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC);
    final boolean isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital;
    if (isPrintOrDisc && item.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
    {
      item.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
    }
    return getItemLines().size();
  }

   *   Finalizes an existing order by transitioning its status to COMPLETED
   *   and then updating the status of each library item that was previously
   *   placed on HOLD (only for PRINT_FORMAT book items and DISC digital
   *   items) to LOAN. Other item types retain their current status.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Set this.status = COMPLETED.
   *   2. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
   *        a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
   *        b. Determine if the item is a print/disc item:
   *             - isPrintBook = (item is BookItem && item.type == PRINT_FORMAT)
   *             - isDiscDigital = (item is DigitalItem && item.option == DISC)
   *             - isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital
   *        c. If isPrintOrDisc AND item.status == HOLD:
   *              - Set item.status = LOAN.
   *        d. Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
   *   3. End.
   * Related Features:
   *   - Order.status (OrderStatus)
   *   - Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
   *   - ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem)
   *   - LibraryItem.status (LibraryItemStatus)
   * Related Classes:
   *   Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus,
   *   LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
   * Input:
   *   (none) - operates on the receiver order's current state.
   * Output:
   *   void
   *     - No return value.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - The receiver Order must be initialized with a non-null itemLines list
   *     and a valid status (typically PENDING, though the operation will set
   *     COMPLETED regardless of current status to satisfy the requirement).
   * Post-condition:
   *   - this.status == COMPLETED.
   *   - For every PRINT_FORMAT book or DISC digital item previously in HOLD
   *     status, its status is now LOAN. All other items retain their previous
   *     status.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void handleOrder()
  {
    setStatus(OrderStatus.COMPLETED);
    for (final ItemLine itemLine : getItemLines())
    {
      final LibraryItem item = itemLine.getLibraryItem();
      final boolean isPrintBook = (item instanceof BookItem) && (((BookItem) item).getType() == BookItemType.PRINT_FORMAT);
      final boolean isDiscDigital = (item instanceof DigitalItem) && (((DigitalItem) item).getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DISC);
      final boolean isPrintOrDisc = isPrintBook || isDiscDigital;
      if (isPrintOrDisc && item.getStatus() == LibraryItemStatus.HOLD)
      {
        item.setStatus(LibraryItemStatus.LOAN);
      }
    }
  }

   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Calculates the total number of downloads for this specific Audio digital
   *   item across all Completed orders. Only orders whose status is COMPLETED
   *   are considered, and only when this digital item's option is DOWNLOADABLE
   *   (not DISC). Returns 0 when there are no qualifying downloads.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.type != AUDIO, return 0 (only audio items are considered).
   *   2. If this.option != DOWNLOADABLE, return 0 (disc items do not count).
   *   3. Initialize total = 0.
   *   4. For each order in orders:
   *        a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip.
   *        b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *              i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this:
   *                    - total += itemLine.quantity
   *   5. Return total.
   * Related Features:
   *   - DigitalItem.option (DigitalItemOption)
   *   - DigitalItem.type (DigitalItemType)
   *   - ItemLine.quantity
   *   - ItemLine.libraryItem
   *   - Order.status (OrderStatus)
   *   - Order.itemLines
   * Related Classes:
   *   DigitalItem, Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, OrderStatus, DigitalItemOption, DigitalItemType
   * Input:
   *   orders : List<Order>
   *     - A non-null list of Order objects to be inspected. May be empty.
   * Output:
   *   int
   *     - The total quantity of this digital item appearing in item lines of
   *       Completed orders, provided this item is an AUDIO item with option
   *       DOWNLOADABLE. Returns 0 if no qualifying downloads exist or if this
   *       item is not eligible (non-audio or disc option).
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - orders must not be null (may be empty).
   *   - this DigitalItem must be a valid object.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - No state is modified. The method returns a non-negative integer count.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model ordersMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    if (getType() != DigitalItemType.AUDIO)
    {
      return 0;
    }
    if (getOption() != DigitalItemOption.DOWNLOADABLE)
    {
      return 0;
    }
    int total = 0;
    for (Order order : orders)
    {
      if (order.getStatus() != OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
      {
        continue;
      }
      for (ItemLine itemLine : order.getItemLines())
      {
        if (itemLine.getLibraryItem() == this)
        {
          total += itemLine.getQuantity();
        }
      }
    }
    return total;
  }

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title olrs Model

class BookItem {
    type : BookItemType
}
class DigitalItem {
    option : DigitalItemOption
    type : DigitalItemType
    calculateTotalDownloads(orders : List<Order>) : int
}
class ItemLine {
    quantity : int
    libraryItem : LibraryItem
}
abstract class LibraryItem {
    status : LibraryItemStatus
}
class Order {
    status : OrderStatus
    itemLines : List<ItemLine>
    addItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : boolean
    countPrintBookItemIfCompleted() : int
    handleOrder() : void
    removeItemLine(itemLine : ItemLine) : int
}
enum BookItemType {
    EBOOK
    PRINT_FORMAT
}
enum DigitalItemOption {
    DOWNLOADABLE
    DISC
}
enum DigitalItemType {
    AUDIO
    VIDEO
}
enum LibraryItemStatus {
    AVAILABLE
    HOLD
    LOAN
}
enum OrderStatus {
    PENDING
    COMPLETED
}

BookItem --|> LibraryItem
DigitalItem --|> LibraryItem
ItemLine --> LibraryItem : libraryItem
Order *-- "*" ItemLine : itemLines
@enduml
```

# Operations
+ DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
+ Order::addItemLine
+ Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
+ Order::handleOrder
+ Order::removeItemLine

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

```plantuml
    DISC
}

    VIDEO
```

# Operation Specifications
DigitalItem::calculateTotalDownloads
Summary: Calculates the total download count for a specific Audio digital item across all completed orders. Only orders with COMPLETED status are considered, and only those digital items whose option is DOWNLOADABLE (not DISC) are counted. The quantity of each qualifying item line is summed to produce the total download count. If no qualifying downloads exist, the method returns 0.
Algorithm:
  1. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
  2. If this.type != AUDIO, return 0.
  3. For each order in orders:
       a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip this order.
       b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
            i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE:
                 - totalDownloads += itemLine.quantity
  4. Return totalDownloads.
Related Features: DigitalItem.type (DigitalItemType), DigitalItem.option (DigitalItemOption)
Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus
Input:
  - orders : List<Order> - a list of Order objects to be searched. Must not be null.
Output:
  - int - the total quantity of downloads for this Audio digital item across all completed orders with DOWNLOADABLE option. Returns 0 if no qualifying downloads exist, or if this digital item is not of type AUDIO.
Pre-condition:
  - The orders list must not be null (may be empty).
  - This digital item must be properly initialized with a valid type and option.
Post-condition:
  - No state is modified; the method is purely a query operation.
  - The returned value reflects the cumulative quantity of this Audio digital item (with DOWNLOADABLE option) across all COMPLETED orders.

# Operation Specifications
Order::countPrintBookItemIfCompleted
Summary: Counts the total number of print-format book items contained within this order, but only if the order's status is COMPLETED. Returns 0 if the order is not completed or if no print-format book items are present.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != COMPLETED, return 0.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
       a. If itemLine.libraryItem is a BookItem AND itemLine.libraryItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
            - count += 1
  4. Return count.
Related Features: Order.status (OrderStatus), Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
Related Features (accessed on related objects): ItemLine.libraryItem, BookItem.type
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, OrderStatus, BookItemType
Input: (none)
Output:
  - int - the number of print-format book items in this order if the order status is COMPLETED; otherwise 0. Returns 0 when the order is not completed or when no qualifying book items exist.
Pre-condition:
  - The order must be properly initialized with a valid status and itemLines list (itemLines may be empty).
Post-condition:
  - No state is modified; the method is a pure query operation.
  - The returned integer represents the count of distinct print-format book item lines in the order (only counted when status is COMPLETED).

Order::removeItemLine
Summary: Removes an item line from an existing order that is in PENDING status. On success, returns the updated count of item lines remaining in the order, and conditionally resets the associated library item's status to AVAILABLE if it was previously HOLD (only for print-format book items or disc digital items). Returns -1 if the order is not in PENDING status (i.e., the removal fails).
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING, return -1.
  2. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
  3. Remove itemLine from this.itemLines.
  4. Determine whether item is a print-format book item or a disc digital item:
       - If item is a BookItem AND item.type == PRINT_FORMAT AND item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
       - Else if item is a DigitalItem AND item.option == DISC AND item.status == HOLD, set item.status = AVAILABLE.
       - Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
  5. Return this.itemLines.size().
Related Features: Order.status (OrderStatus), Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
Related Features (accessed on related objects): ItemLine.libraryItem, LibraryItem.status, BookItem.type, DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input:
  - itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to be removed from the order. Must not be null and must be present in this.itemLines.
Output:
  - int - the updated count of item lines remaining in this order after successful removal; returns -1 if the order is not in PENDING status (removal failed).
Pre-condition:
  - The order must exist and have status == PENDING.
  - The itemLine parameter must not be null and must be a member of this.itemLines.
Post-condition:
  - If -1 is returned: no state changes occur; the order's status remains non-PENDING and the item line list is unchanged.
  - If a non-negative integer is returned: the item line has been removed from this.itemLines, and the associated library item's status has been reset to AVAILABLE only if it was a print-format book item or a disc digital item with previous status HOLD; otherwise the library item's status is unchanged.

Order::handleOrder
Summary: Processes an existing order by transitioning it from PENDING to COMPLETED status, and then updates the status of each associated library item from HOLD to LOAN, but only for items that are print-format book items or disc digital items. Other items retain their current status.
Algorithm:
  1. Set this.status = COMPLETED.
  2. For each itemLine in this.itemLines:
       a. Let item = itemLine.libraryItem.
       b. If item is a BookItem AND item.type == PRINT_FORMAT:
            - If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
       c. Else if item is a DigitalItem AND item.option == DISC:
            - If item.status == HOLD, set item.status = LOAN.
       d. Otherwise, leave item.status unchanged.
  3. End.
Related Features: Order.status (OrderStatus), Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
Related Features (accessed on related objects): ItemLine.libraryItem, LibraryItem.status, BookItem.type, DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input: (none)
Output: void - no value is returned.
Pre-condition:
  - The order must exist and be properly initialized.
  - The order is expected to be in PENDING status (the operation transitions it to COMPLETED).
  - All library items referenced by the order's item lines must be properly initialized.
Post-condition:
  - this.status is set to COMPLETED.
  - For each item line whose library item is a print-format book item or a disc digital item and whose status was HOLD, the library item's status is updated to LOAN.
  - All other library items retain their original status unchanged.

Order::addItemLine
Summary: Adds a new item line to the order, enforcing business rules regarding order status, item availability, duplicate prevention, and conditional status updates of the library item. Returns true if the item line was successfully added; returns false if any validation rule fails.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.status != PENDING, return false.
  2. Let newItem = itemLine.libraryItem.
  3. If newItem.status != AVAILABLE, return false.
  4. For each existing itemLine in this.itemLines:
       a. If existingItemLine.libraryItem == newItem, return false (duplicate detected).
  5. Add itemLine to this.itemLines.
  6. Determine whether newItem is a print-format book item or a disc digital item:
       - If newItem is a BookItem AND newItem.type == PRINT_FORMAT, set newItem.status = HOLD.
       - Else if newItem is a DigitalItem AND newItem.option == DISC, set newItem.status = HOLD.
       - Otherwise, leave newItem.status as AVAILABLE.
  7. Return true.
Related Features: Order.status (OrderStatus), Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>)
Related Features (accessed on related objects): ItemLine.libraryItem, LibraryItem.status, BookItem.type, DigitalItem.option
Related Classes: ItemLine, LibraryItem, BookItem, DigitalItem, OrderStatus, LibraryItemStatus, BookItemType, DigitalItemOption
Input:
  - itemLine : ItemLine - the item line to be added. Must not be null. Must reference a valid LibraryItem via its libraryItem attribute. The quantity field is assumed to be valid (>= 1).
Output:
  - boolean - true if the item line was successfully added (all validations passed and item line was appended to the order); false if validation failed (order not pending, item not available, or duplicate item line detected).
Pre-condition:
  - The order must exist and have status == PENDING.
  - The itemLine parameter must not be null.
  - The libraryItem referenced by itemLine must have status == AVAILABLE.
  - The libraryItem must not already appear in any existing item line of this order.
Post-condition:
  - If true is returned: the item line has been added to this.itemLines, and the libraryItem's status has been updated to HOLD if it is a print-format book item or a disc digital item; otherwise, the libraryItem's status remains AVAILABLE.
  - If false is returned: no state changes occur in the order or the library item.

You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

# Requirement
```text
Online Library System (OLS) provides easy access to customers to order different library items. A customer can place multiple orders. The status of an order can be pending or completed. Furthermore, each order is composed of multiple item lines. Each item line has a quantity. An item line is associated with a library item. The library item can be a book item or a digital item. Each library item has a status. The status can be available, hold, or loan. The book item can be of types - eBook format or print format. On the other side, the digital item can be an audio item or a video item. In addition, a digital item also has two options - downloadable and disc.
Functional requirement:
1. Add a new item line to order. Preconditions. The order must be in Pending status; the library item must be available (not Hold or Loan). Ensure no duplicate item line (same library item) exists in the order. And modify the library item's status from available to hold only if the library item is a print format book item or disc digital item. Otherwise, keep the status as Available. Return true if item line added, false if validation fails.
2. Remove an item line from an existing order in Pending status. If successful: Return the updated count of item lines in the order. Reset the library item's status to available only if it was previously Hold (for print/disc items). Otherwise, retain its original status. If failed (order is completed): Return -1.
3. Handle an existing order. First, update Order Status to "completed". And then modify the library item's status from hold to loan only if the library item belongs to a print format book item or disc digital item.
4. Calculate the total downloads for a specific Audio digital item. Only consider orders with Completed status. Only count if the digital item's option is Downloadable (not Disc). Return total download count. If no download, return 0.
5. Count the total number of print-format book items in a given order only if the order status is completed. Return 0 if none.
```

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.

  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary: Calculates the total download count for a specific Audio digital item across all completed orders. Only orders with COMPLETED status are considered, and only those digital items whose option is DOWNLOADABLE (not DISC) are counted. The quantity of each qualifying item line is summed to produce the total download count. If no qualifying downloads exist, the method returns 0.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. Initialize totalDownloads = 0.
   *   2. If this.type != AUDIO, return 0.
   *   3. For each order in orders:
   *        a. If order.status != COMPLETED, skip this order.
   *        b. For each itemLine in order.itemLines:
   *             i. If itemLine.libraryItem == this AND this.option == DOWNLOADABLE:
   *                  - totalDownloads += itemLine.quantity
   *   4. Return totalDownloads.
   * Related Features: DigitalItem.type (DigitalItemType), DigitalItem.option (DigitalItemOption), Order.status (OrderStatus), Order.itemLines (List<ItemLine>), ItemLine.libraryItem (LibraryItem), ItemLine.quantity (int)
   * Related Classes: Order, ItemLine, LibraryItem, DigitalItemType, DigitalItemOption, OrderStatus
   * Input:
   *   - orders : List<Order> - a list of Order objects to be searched. Must not be null.
   * Output:
   *   - int - the total quantity of downloads for this Audio digital item across all completed orders with DOWNLOADABLE option. Returns 0 if no qualifying downloads exist, or if this digital item is not of type AUDIO.
```

   * @generated NOT

  public int calculateTotalDownloads(final EList<Order> orders)
  {
    int totalDownloads = 0;
    if (getType() != DigitalItemType.AUDIO)
    {
      return 0;
    }
    for (Order order : orders)
    {
      if (order.getStatus() != OrderStatus.COMPLETED)
      {
        continue;
      }
      for (ItemLine itemLine : order.getItemLines())
      {
        if (itemLine.getLibraryItem() == this && getOption() == DigitalItemOption.DOWNLOADABLE)
        {
          totalDownloads += itemLine.getQuantity();
        }
      }
    }
    return totalDownloads;
  }
